===FILE: res://scripts/MinecraftRig.gd===
@tool
class_name MinecraftRig
extends Node3D

enum SkinModel {
	CLASSIC,
	SLIM,
}

enum PivotMode {
	CENTER,
	TOP,
	BOTTOM,
}

const PX := 1.0 / 16.0
const BODY_OUTER_INFLATE_PX := 0.25
const HEAD_OUTER_INFLATE_PX := 0.5

var _skin_model: SkinModel = SkinModel.CLASSIC
@export var skin_model: SkinModel:
	get:
		return _skin_model
	set(value):
		if _skin_model == value:
			return
		_skin_model = value
		_queue_rebuild()

var _skin_path: String = "res://skin.png"
@export_file("*.png") var skin_path: String:
	get:
		return _skin_path
	set(value):
		if _skin_path == value:
			return
		_skin_path = value
		_queue_rebuild()

var _show_outer_layer := true
@export var show_outer_layer := true:
	get:
		return _show_outer_layer
	set(value):
		_show_outer_layer = value
		_apply_outer_visibility()

@export var rebuild_now := false:
	set(value):
		if value:
			_queue_rebuild()
		rebuild_now = false

var material_inner: StandardMaterial3D
var material_outer: StandardMaterial3D

var model_root: Node3D

var head: Node3D
var body: Node3D
var arm_l: Node3D
var arm_r: Node3D
var leg_l: Node3D
var leg_r: Node3D

var _head_rest_position := Vector3.ZERO
var _body_rest_position := Vector3.ZERO
var _arm_l_rest_position := Vector3.ZERO
var _arm_r_rest_position := Vector3.ZERO
var _leg_l_rest_position := Vector3.ZERO
var _leg_r_rest_position := Vector3.ZERO

var _idle_time := 0.0
var _walk_time := 0.0
var _smoothed_speed := 0.0
var _pose_ready := false

var _outer_meshes: Array[MeshInstance3D] = []
var _rebuild_queued := false

func _ready() -> void:
	# Clean up any statically saved legacy meshes from the previous script run!
	for child in get_children():
		child.queue_free()
	
	_queue_rebuild()

func _queue_rebuild() -> void:
	if not is_inside_tree():
		return
	if _rebuild_queued:
		return
	_rebuild_queued = true
	call_deferred("_rebuild")

func _rebuild() -> void:
	_rebuild_queued = false
	_ensure_materials()
	_ensure_model_root()
	_clear_model_root()

	var skin_texture := _load_skin_texture(_skin_path)
	material_inner.albedo_texture = skin_texture
	material_outer.albedo_texture = skin_texture

	_build_parts()
	_cache_rest_pose()
	_apply_outer_visibility()

func _ensure_materials() -> void:
	material_inner = StandardMaterial3D.new()
	material_inner.texture_filter = BaseMaterial3D.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	material_inner.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	material_inner.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_DISABLED
	material_inner.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED
	material_inner.albedo_color = Color.WHITE

	material_outer = StandardMaterial3D.new()
	material_outer.texture_filter = BaseMaterial3D.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	material_outer.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	material_outer.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA_SCISSOR
	material_outer.alpha_scissor_threshold = 0.5
	material_outer.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_BACK
	material_outer.albedo_color = Color.WHITE

func _ensure_model_root() -> void:
	if model_root != null and is_instance_valid(model_root):
		return

	model_root = Node3D.new()
	model_root.name = "ModelRoot"
	add_child(model_root)

func _clear_model_root() -> void:
	_outer_meshes.clear()
	_pose_ready = false

	for child in model_root.get_children():
		child.free()

	head = null
	body = null
	arm_l = null
	arm_r = null
	leg_l = null
	leg_r = null

func _build_parts() -> void:
	var arm_width_px := 3.0 if _skin_model == SkinModel.SLIM else 4.0
	var shoulder_x_px := 4.0 + arm_width_px * 0.5

	leg_r = MinecraftRigMeshBuilder.create_part(
		model_root,
		_outer_meshes,
		material_inner,
		material_outer,
		"RightLeg",
		Vector3(4, 12, 4),
		MinecraftSkinUv.right_leg_uv(true),
		MinecraftSkinUv.right_leg_uv(false),
		PivotMode.TOP,
		BODY_OUTER_INFLATE_PX
	)
	leg_r.position = Vector3(-2.0 * PX, 12.0 * PX, 0.0)

	leg_l = MinecraftRigMeshBuilder.create_part(
		model_root,
		_outer_meshes,
		material_inner,
		material_outer,
		"LeftLeg",
		Vector3(4, 12, 4),
		MinecraftSkinUv.left_leg_uv(true),
		MinecraftSkinUv.left_leg_uv(false),
		PivotMode.TOP,
		BODY_OUTER_INFLATE_PX
	)
	leg_l.position = Vector3(2.0 * PX, 12.0 * PX, 0.0)

	body = MinecraftRigMeshBuilder.create_part(
		model_root,
		_outer_meshes,
		material_inner,
		material_outer,
		"Body",
		Vector3(8, 12, 4),
		MinecraftSkinUv.body_uv(true),
		MinecraftSkinUv.body_uv(false),
		PivotMode.CENTER,
		BODY_OUTER_INFLATE_PX
	)
	body.position = Vector3(0.0, 18.0 * PX, 0.0)

	head = MinecraftRigMeshBuilder.create_part(
		model_root,
		_outer_meshes,
		material_inner,
		material_outer,
		"Head",
		Vector3(8, 8, 8),
		MinecraftSkinUv.head_uv(true),
		MinecraftSkinUv.head_uv(false),
		PivotMode.BOTTOM,
		HEAD_OUTER_INFLATE_PX
	)
	head.position = Vector3(0.0, 24.0 * PX, 0.0)

	arm_r = MinecraftRigMeshBuilder.create_part(
		model_root,
		_outer_meshes,
		material_inner,
		material_outer,
		"RightArm",
		Vector3(arm_width_px, 12, 4),
		MinecraftSkinUv.right_arm_uv(true, _skin_model == SkinModel.SLIM),
		MinecraftSkinUv.right_arm_uv(false, _skin_model == SkinModel.SLIM),
		PivotMode.TOP,
		BODY_OUTER_INFLATE_PX
	)
	arm_r.position = Vector3(-shoulder_x_px * PX, 24.0 * PX, 0.0)

	arm_l = MinecraftRigMeshBuilder.create_part(
		model_root,
		_outer_meshes,
		material_inner,
		material_outer,
		"LeftArm",
		Vector3(arm_width_px, 12, 4),
		MinecraftSkinUv.left_arm_uv(true, _skin_model == SkinModel.SLIM),
		MinecraftSkinUv.left_arm_uv(false, _skin_model == SkinModel.SLIM),
		PivotMode.TOP,
		BODY_OUTER_INFLATE_PX
	)
	arm_l.position = Vector3(shoulder_x_px * PX, 24.0 * PX, 0.0)

func _cache_rest_pose() -> void:
	if head == null or body == null or arm_l == null or arm_r == null or leg_l == null or leg_r == null:
		_pose_ready = false
		return

	_head_rest_position = head.position
	_body_rest_position = body.position
	_arm_l_rest_position = arm_l.position
	_arm_r_rest_position = arm_r.position
	_leg_l_rest_position = leg_l.position
	_leg_r_rest_position = leg_r.position

	head.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	body.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	arm_l.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	arm_r.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	leg_l.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	leg_r.rotation = Vector3.ZERO

	_pose_ready = true

func _animate_part(part: Node3D, target_rotation: Vector3, target_position: Vector3, blend: float) -> void:
	part.rotation.x = lerp_angle(part.rotation.x, target_rotation.x, blend)
	part.rotation.y = lerp_angle(part.rotation.y, target_rotation.y, blend)
	part.rotation.z = lerp_angle(part.rotation.z, target_rotation.z, blend)
	part.position = part.position.lerp(target_position, blend)

func load_skin_from_file(file_path: String) -> bool:
	var image := Image.load_from_file(file_path)
	if image == null or image.is_empty():
		push_warning("Could not load image: %s" % file_path)
		return false

	if image.get_width() != 64 or image.get_height() != 64:
		push_warning("Minecraft skin must be 64x64.")
		return false

	_skin_path = file_path
	var texture := ImageTexture.create_from_image(image)
	_apply_texture(texture)
	return true

func _load_skin_texture(path: String) -> Texture2D:
	return MinecraftRigSkinResources.load_skin_texture(path)

func _apply_texture(texture: Texture2D) -> void:
	if material_inner == null or material_outer == null:
		return
	material_inner.albedo_texture = texture
	material_outer.albedo_texture = texture

func _apply_outer_visibility() -> void:
	for mesh in _outer_meshes:
		if is_instance_valid(mesh):
			mesh.visible = _show_outer_layer

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if Engine.is_editor_hint():
		return
	if not is_instance_valid(get_parent()):
		return
	if not _pose_ready:
		return
	if head == null or body == null or arm_l == null or arm_r == null or leg_l == null or leg_r == null:
		return

	var velocity_value = get_parent().get("velocity")
	if typeof(velocity_value) != TYPE_VECTOR3:
		return

	var velocity: Vector3 = velocity_value
	var horizontal_velocity: Vector3 = Vector3(velocity.x, 0.0, velocity.z)
	var horizontal_speed: float = horizontal_velocity.length()

	_idle_time += delta
	_smoothed_speed = lerpf(_smoothed_speed, horizontal_speed, min(1.0, delta * 10.0))

	var speed_ratio: float = clamp(_smoothed_speed / 6.0, 0.0, 1.0)
	var move_weight: float = clamp(_smoothed_speed / 3.5, 0.0, 1.0)
	var idle_weight: float = 1.0 - move_weight

	_walk_time += delta * lerpf(2.0, 10.5, speed_ratio)

	var local_velocity: Vector3 = get_parent().global_transform.basis.inverse() * horizontal_velocity
	var walk_direction: float = 1.0
	if local_velocity.z > 0.05:
		walk_direction = -1.0

	var strafe_amount: float = 0.0
	if _smoothed_speed > 0.001:
		strafe_amount = clamp(local_velocity.x / _smoothed_speed, -1.0, 1.0)

	var walk_sin: float = sin(_walk_time)
	var walk_cos: float = cos(_walk_time)
	var step_bounce: float = abs(walk_cos)

	var idle_bob: float = sin(_idle_time * 2.0) * 0.02 * idle_weight
	var idle_breath: float = sin(_idle_time * 1.6) * 0.025 * idle_weight
	var idle_sway: float = sin(_idle_time * 1.1) * 0.03 * idle_weight

	var body_position := _body_rest_position + Vector3(0.0, idle_bob + step_bounce * 0.025 * move_weight, 0.0)
	var head_position := _head_rest_position + Vector3(0.0, idle_bob * 1.2 + step_bounce * 0.015 * move_weight, 0.0)
	var arm_l_position := _arm_l_rest_position + Vector3(0.0, idle_bob * 0.35, 0.0)
	var arm_r_position := _arm_r_rest_position + Vector3(0.0, idle_bob * 0.35, 0.0)
	var leg_l_position := _leg_l_rest_position
	var leg_r_position := _leg_r_rest_position

	var arm_swing: float = walk_sin * 0.95 * move_weight * walk_direction
	var leg_swing: float = walk_sin * 1.2 * move_weight * walk_direction
	var body_roll: float = (-strafe_amount * 0.12 * move_weight) + idle_sway * 0.25
	var head_roll: float = -idle_sway * 0.18
	var arm_yaw: float = strafe_amount * 0.08 * move_weight
	var leg_roll: float = strafe_amount * 0.06 * move_weight

	var body_rotation := Vector3(
		idle_breath + step_bounce * 0.04 * move_weight,
		0.0,
		body_roll
	)
	var head_rotation := Vector3(
		idle_breath * 0.8 - step_bounce * 0.02 * move_weight,
		arm_yaw * 0.8,
		head_roll
	)
	var arm_l_rotation: float = arm_swing
	var arm_r_rotation: float = -arm_swing
	var leg_l_rotation: float = -leg_swing
	var leg_r_rotation: float = leg_swing

	if move_weight < 0.05:
		arm_l_rotation = 0.0
		arm_r_rotation = 0.0
		leg_l_rotation = 0.0
		leg_r_rotation = 0.0
		body_rotation = Vector3(idle_breath, 0.0, idle_sway * 0.25)
		head_rotation = Vector3(idle_breath * 0.8, 0.0, -idle_sway * 0.18)

	var blend: float = min(1.0, delta * lerpf(6.0, 14.0, move_weight))

	_animate_part(body, body_rotation, body_position, blend)
	_animate_part(head, head_rotation, head_position, blend)
	_animate_part(arm_l, Vector3(arm_l_rotation, arm_yaw, -0.08 if move_weight >= 0.05 else 0.0), arm_l_position, blend)
	_animate_part(arm_r, Vector3(arm_r_rotation, arm_yaw, 0.08 if move_weight >= 0.05 else 0.0), arm_r_position, blend)
	_animate_part(leg_l, Vector3(leg_l_rotation, 0.0, leg_roll), leg_l_position, blend)
	_animate_part(leg_r, Vector3(leg_r_rotation, 0.0, leg_roll), leg_r_position, blend)
===END_FILE===

@tool
extends Node3D

@export var generate_floor_now: bool = false:
	set(value):
		# We act as a button: When you click this checkbox in the inspector, it runs!
		_generate_ground()

func _generate_ground():
	print("Generating 10x10 blocks in viewport!")
	
	# Clear existing blocks first so we don't accidentally double-stack them
	for child in get_children():
		if child is CSGBox3D and child.name.begins_with("GrassBlock_"):
			child.free()
	
	var mat = StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = Color(0.3, 0.7, 0.3) # Grass
	
	var idx = 0
	for x in range(-5, 5):
		for z in range(-5, 5):
			var block = CSGBox3D.new()
			block.name = "GrassBlock_%d" % idx
			block.size = Vector3(1, 1, 1)
			block.position = Vector3(x + 0.5, -0.5, z + 0.5)
			block.material = mat
			add_child(block)
			
			if Engine.is_editor_hint():
				# Must assign the owner to the current edited root so Godot saves it into the scene!
				block.owner = get_tree().edited_scene_root
			idx += 1

===FILE: res://scripts/camera/room_view_camera_controller.gd===
class_name RoomViewCameraController
extends Node3D

const DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION := Vector3(0.0, 0.9, 0.0)
const DEFAULT_CAMERA_POSITION := Vector3(11.8, 9.6, 11.2)

@export var target_position: Vector3 = DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
@export_range(0.001, 0.03, 0.001) var orbit_sensitivity: float = 0.01
@export_range(1.0, 30.0, 0.1) var orbit_smoothing: float = 14.0
@export_range(0.1, 30.0, 0.1) var orbit_inertia_damping: float = 9.5
@export_range(1.0, 300.0, 1.0) var orbit_throw_strength: float = 110.0
@export_range(-1.2, 1.4, 0.01) var min_pitch: float = -0.75
@export_range(0.2, 1.5, 0.01) var max_pitch: float = 1.5
@export_range(2.0, 48.0, 0.1) var min_distance: float = 5.0
@export_range(2.0, 48.0, 0.1) var max_distance: float = 48.0
@export_range(0.0001, 0.01, 0.0001) var wheel_sensitivity: float = 0.0015
@export_range(1.0, 30.0, 0.1) var zoom_smoothing: float = 14.0

var _yaw: float = 0.0
var _pitch: float = 0.0
var _target_yaw: float = 0.0
var _target_pitch: float = 0.0
var _yaw_velocity: float = 0.0
var _pitch_velocity: float = 0.0
var _current_distance: float = 0.0
var _target_distance: float = 0.0
var _drag_button: MouseButton = MOUSE_BUTTON_NONE

@onready var camera: Camera3D = $Camera3D

func _ready() -> void:
	reset_camera()
	if camera != null:
		camera.current = true
	_sync_camera_transform()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if camera == null:
		return

	if _drag_button == MOUSE_BUTTON_NONE:
		_target_yaw = wrapf(_target_yaw + _yaw_velocity * delta, -PI, PI)
		_target_pitch = clamp(_target_pitch + _pitch_velocity * delta, min_pitch, max_pitch)

		var orbit_damping := exp(-orbit_inertia_damping * delta)
		_yaw_velocity *= orbit_damping
		_pitch_velocity *= orbit_damping
		if absf(_yaw_velocity) < 0.0001:
			_yaw_velocity = 0.0
		if absf(_pitch_velocity) < 0.0001:
			_pitch_velocity = 0.0

	var orbit_blend: float = min(1.0, delta * orbit_smoothing)
	_yaw = lerp_angle(_yaw, _target_yaw, orbit_blend)
	_pitch = lerpf(_pitch, _target_pitch, orbit_blend)

	var zoom_blend: float = min(1.0, delta * zoom_smoothing)
	_current_distance = lerpf(_current_distance, _target_distance, zoom_blend)
	_sync_camera_transform()

func handle_input_event(event: InputEvent) -> bool:
	if camera == null:
		return false

	if event is InputEventMouseButton:
		var mouse_button := event as InputEventMouseButton
		match mouse_button.button_index:
			MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
				if mouse_button.pressed:
					_apply_zoom_delta(-1.0, absf(mouse_button.factor))
					return true
			MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
				if mouse_button.pressed:
					_apply_zoom_delta(1.0, absf(mouse_button.factor))
					return true
			MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE, MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT:
				if mouse_button.pressed:
					if get_viewport().gui_get_hovered_control() != null:
						return false
					_drag_button = mouse_button.button_index
					return true
				if _drag_button == mouse_button.button_index:
					_drag_button = MOUSE_BUTTON_NONE
					return true
		return false

	if event is InputEventMouseMotion and (
		_drag_button == MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE or _drag_button == MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT
	):
		var motion := event as InputEventMouseMotion
		var yaw_delta: float = -motion.relative.x * orbit_sensitivity
		var pitch_delta: float = motion.relative.y * orbit_sensitivity
		_target_yaw = wrapf(_target_yaw + yaw_delta, -PI, PI)
		_target_pitch = clamp(_target_pitch + pitch_delta, min_pitch, max_pitch)
		_yaw_velocity = yaw_delta * orbit_throw_strength
		_pitch_velocity = pitch_delta * orbit_throw_strength
		return true

	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		var key_event := event as InputEventKey
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_HOME:
			reset_camera()
			return true

	return false

func get_camera() -> Camera3D:
	return camera

func get_target_global_position() -> Vector3:
	return global_position + target_position

func get_planar_forward() -> Vector3:
	if camera == null:
		return Vector3.FORWARD

	var forward := get_target_global_position() - camera.global_position
	forward.y = 0.0
	if forward.length_squared() <= 0.0001:
		return Vector3.FORWARD
	return forward.normalized()

func get_planar_right() -> Vector3:
	var forward := get_planar_forward()
	var right := forward.cross(Vector3.UP)
	if right.length_squared() <= 0.0001:
		return Vector3.RIGHT
	return right.normalized()

func reset_camera() -> void:
	var default_offset := DEFAULT_CAMERA_POSITION - DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
	_yaw = atan2(default_offset.x, default_offset.z)
	_pitch = clamp(asin(default_offset.y / default_offset.length()), min_pitch, max_pitch)
	_target_yaw = _yaw
	_target_pitch = _pitch
	_yaw_velocity = 0.0
	_pitch_velocity = 0.0
	_current_distance = clamp(default_offset.length(), min_distance, max_distance)
	_target_distance = _current_distance
	_drag_button = MOUSE_BUTTON_NONE
	_sync_camera_transform()

func _apply_zoom_delta(direction: float, magnitude: float = 0.0) -> void:
	var zoom_amount: float = maxf(magnitude, 1.0)
	var zoom_scale: float = exp(direction * zoom_amount * wheel_sensitivity * 100.0)
	_target_distance = clamp(_target_distance * zoom_scale, min_distance, max_distance)

func _sync_camera_transform() -> void:
	if camera == null:
		return

	var target_global := get_target_global_position()
	var orbit_radius := cos(_pitch) * _current_distance
	var camera_offset := Vector3(
		sin(_yaw) * orbit_radius,
		sin(_pitch) * _current_distance,
		cos(_yaw) * orbit_radius
	)

	camera.global_position = target_global + camera_offset
	camera.look_at(target_global, Vector3.UP)
===END_FILE===

@export var room_shell_path: NodePath

var _items_root: Node3D
var _wall_panel_root: Node3D
var _ceiling_plate_root: Node3D
var _item_view_camera: Camera3D
var _free_camera: Camera3D
var _ui_layer: CanvasLayer
var _ui_root: Control
var _panel: PanelContainer
var _status_label: Label
var _search_input: LineEdit
var _mount_filter_option: OptionButton
var _category_filter_option: OptionButton
var _collision_toggle_button: Button
var _footprint_toggle_button: Button
var _support_toggle_button: Button
var _anchor_toggle_button: Button
var _label_toggle_button: Button
var _selection_label: Label
var _return_button: Button
var _previous_item_button: Button
var _next_item_button: Button
var _free_camera_button: Button
var _item_index_label: Label

var _item_records: Array[Dictionary] = []
var _item_record_lookup: Dictionary = {}
var _selected_item_index := -1
var _free_camera_active := false
var _free_camera_looking := false
var _free_camera_yaw := -0.78
var _free_camera_pitch := -0.42
var _free_camera_move_speed := FREE_CAMERA_BASE_SPEED

func _ready() -> void:
	if Engine.is_editor_hint():
		return

	_room_shell = get_node_or_null(room_shell_path) as RoomShell
	_placement_manager = get_node_or_null(placement_manager_path) as PlacementManager
	_room_camera_controller = get_node_or_null(room_camera_controller_path) as RoomViewCameraController
	_player = get_node_or_null(player_path)
	if _room_camera_controller != null:
		_saved_camera_target = _room_camera_controller.target_position
		_has_saved_camera_target = true

	_build_showroom()
	_build_ui()
	_populate_filter_options()
	_set_showroom_visible(false)
	set_process(true)

func is_debug_world_enabled() -> bool:
	return _debug_world_enabled

func set_debug_world_enabled(enabled: bool) -> void:
	if _debug_world_enabled == enabled:
		return

	_debug_world_enabled = enabled
	if _debug_world_enabled:
		_apply_external_mode_state(true)
		if _showroom_dirty or _item_records.is_empty():
			_rebuild_showroom_items()
		_set_showroom_visible(true)
		_set_free_camera_active(false)
		_focus_showroom_camera()
	else:
		_set_free_camera_active(false)
		_set_showroom_visible(false)
		_apply_external_mode_state(false)
		_restore_room_camera()
		_set_selected_item(null)

	debug_world_enabled_changed.emit(_debug_world_enabled)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not _debug_world_enabled or not _free_camera_active or _free_camera == null:
		return
	if _search_input != null and _search_input.has_focus():
		return

	var forward := -_free_camera.global_basis.z
	var right := _free_camera.global_basis.x
	var up := Vector3.UP
	var move_direction := Vector3.ZERO
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_W):
		move_direction += forward
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_S):
		move_direction -= forward
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_D):
		move_direction += right
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_A):
		move_direction -= right
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_E):
		move_direction += up
	if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_Q):
		move_direction -= up
	if move_direction.length_squared() <= 0.0001:
		return

	move_direction = move_direction.normalized()
	var speed_multiplier := FREE_CAMERA_FAST_MULTIPLIER if Input.is_key_pressed(KEY_SHIFT) else 1.0
	_free_camera.global_position += move_direction * _free_camera_move_speed * speed_multiplier * delta

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not _debug_world_enabled or event == null:
		return

	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
		var key_event := event as InputEventKey
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
			set_debug_world_enabled(false)
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_LEFT:
			_focus_relative_item(-1)
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_RIGHT:
			_focus_relative_item(1)
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	if event is InputEventMouseButton:
		var mouse_button := event as InputEventMouseButton
		if _free_camera_active:
			match mouse_button.button_index:
				MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT:
					if _is_pointer_over_ui() and mouse_button.pressed:
						return
					_free_camera_looking = mouse_button.pressed
					get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
					return
				MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
					if mouse_button.pressed:
						_free_camera_move_speed = clampf(_free_camera_move_speed + 1.5, 2.5, 42.0)
						get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
						return
				MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
					if mouse_button.pressed:
						_free_camera_move_speed = clampf(_free_camera_move_speed - 1.5, 2.5, 42.0)
						get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
						return
		if mouse_button.button_index != MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT or not mouse_button.pressed:
			return
		if _is_pointer_over_ui():
			return
		var picked_item := _pick_item_from_mouse(mouse_button.position)
		_set_selected_item(picked_item)
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

	if event is InputEventMouseMotion and _free_camera_active and _free_camera_looking:
		var motion := event as InputEventMouseMotion
		_free_camera_yaw -= motion.relative.x * FREE_CAMERA_LOOK_SENSITIVITY
		_free_camera_pitch = clampf(_free_camera_pitch - motion.relative.y * FREE_CAMERA_LOOK_SENSITIVITY, -1.35, 1.25)
		_sync_free_camera_rotation()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

func _build_showroom() -> void:
	_showroom_root = Node3D.new()
	_showroom_root.name = "DebugShowroom"
	add_child(_showroom_root)

	_static_geometry_root = Node3D.new()
	_static_geometry_root.name = "StaticGeometry"
	_showroom_root.add_child(_static_geometry_root)

	_dynamic_geometry_root = Node3D.new()
	_dynamic_geometry_root.name = "DynamicGeometry"
	_showroom_root.add_child(_dynamic_geometry_root)

	_items_root = Node3D.new()
	_items_root.name = "ShowroomItems"
	_showroom_root.add_child(_items_root)

	_item_view_camera = Camera3D.new()
	_item_view_camera.name = "ItemViewCamera"
	_item_view_camera.projection = Camera3D.PROJECTION_ORTHOGONAL
	_item_view_camera.near = FREE_CAMERA_NEAR
	_item_view_camera.far = FREE_CAMERA_FAR
	_item_view_camera.current = false
	_showroom_root.add_child(_item_view_camera)

	_free_camera = Camera3D.new()
	_free_camera.name = "FreeCamera"
	_free_camera.projection = Camera3D.PROJECTION_PERSPECTIVE
	_free_camera.near = FREE_CAMERA_NEAR
	_free_camera.far = FREE_CAMERA_FAR
	_free_camera.fov = 64.0
	_free_camera.current = false
	_showroom_root.add_child(_free_camera)
	_free_camera.global_position = global_position + SHOWROOM_CAMERA_TARGET_LOCAL + Vector3(12.0, 10.0, 14.0)
	_sync_free_camera_rotation()

	_build_checkerboard_floor()
	_build_wall_panel()
	_build_ceiling_plate()
	_build_zone_labels()

func _build_checkerboard_floor() -> void:
	var floor_root := Node3D.new()
	floor_root.name = "ShowroomFloor"
	floor_root.position = Vector3(0.0, -SHOWROOM_FLOOR_THICKNESS * 0.5, 0.0)
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(floor_root)

	var floor_visual := MeshInstance3D.new()
	floor_visual.name = "Visual"
	var floor_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	floor_mesh.size = Vector3(
		SHOWROOM_FLOOR_HALF_EXTENTS.x * 2.0,
		SHOWROOM_FLOOR_THICKNESS,
		SHOWROOM_FLOOR_HALF_EXTENTS.y * 2.0
	)
	floor_visual.mesh = floor_mesh
	floor_root.add_child(floor_visual)
	RoomFloorMaterials.apply_floor_material(floor_visual, RoomShell.FloorStyle.CHECKERBOARD, SHOWROOM_FLOOR_HALF_EXTENTS)

func _build_wall_panel() -> void:
	_wall_panel_root = Node3D.new()
	_wall_panel_root.name = "WallDebugPanel"
	_wall_panel_root.position = Vector3(0.0, WALL_PANEL_CENTER_Y, WALL_SECTION_SURFACE_Z - WALL_PANEL_THICKNESS * 0.5)
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(_wall_panel_root)

	var panel_visual := MeshInstance3D.new()
	panel_visual.name = "Visual"
	var panel_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	panel_mesh.size = Vector3(WALL_PANEL_WIDTH, WALL_PANEL_HEIGHT, WALL_PANEL_THICKNESS)
	panel_visual.mesh = panel_mesh
	panel_visual.material_override = _make_solid_material(Color(0.78, 0.74, 0.69, 1.0), 0.96)
	_wall_panel_root.add_child(panel_visual)

func _build_ceiling_plate() -> void:
	_ceiling_plate_root = Node3D.new()
	_ceiling_plate_root.name = "CeilingDebugPlate"
	_ceiling_plate_root.position = Vector3(
		0.0,
		CEILING_SURFACE_Y + CEILING_PLATE_THICKNESS * 0.5,
		CEILING_SECTION_CENTER_Z
	)
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(_ceiling_plate_root)

	var ceiling_visual := MeshInstance3D.new()
	ceiling_visual.name = "Visual"
	var ceiling_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	ceiling_mesh.size = Vector3(
		CEILING_PLATE_SIZE.x,
		CEILING_PLATE_THICKNESS,
		CEILING_PLATE_SIZE.y
	)
	ceiling_visual.mesh = ceiling_mesh
	ceiling_visual.material_override = _make_solid_material(Color(0.88, 0.84, 0.78, 0.92), 0.98)
	_ceiling_plate_root.add_child(ceiling_visual)

func _build_zone_labels() -> void:
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(_build_zone_label("Floor Items", Vector3(0.0, 0.2, FLOOR_SECTION_CENTER_Z - 16.4)))
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(_build_zone_label("Wall Items", Vector3(0.0, WALL_PANEL_HEIGHT + 0.9, WALL_SECTION_SURFACE_Z + 0.5)))
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(_build_zone_label("Ceiling Items", Vector3(0.0, CEILING_SURFACE_Y + 0.9, CEILING_SECTION_CENTER_Z - 7.8)))
	_static_geometry_root.add_child(_build_zone_label("Surface Items", Vector3(0.0, SURFACE_STAND_HEIGHT + 1.3, SURFACE_SECTION_CENTER_Z - 9.0)))

func _build_zone_label(text_value: String, local_position: Vector3) -> Label3D:
	var label := Label3D.new()
	label.text = text_value
	label.position = local_position
	label.font_size = 40
	label.pixel_size = 0.012
	label.modulate = PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT
	label.billboard = LABEL_BILLBOARD
	label.outline_size = 10
	return label

func _build_ui() -> void:
	_ui_layer = CanvasLayer.new()
	_ui_layer.name = "DebugWorldUi"
	_ui_layer.layer = 18
	add_child(_ui_layer)

	_ui_root = Control.new()
	_ui_root.name = "Root"
	_ui_root.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	_ui_root.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_ui_layer.add_child(_ui_root)

	_panel = PanelContainer.new()
	_panel.name = "DebugWorldPanel"
	_panel.anchor_left = 0.0
	_panel.anchor_top = 0.0
	_panel.offset_left = UI_MARGIN
	_panel.offset_top = UI_MARGIN
	_panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(UI_PANEL_WIDTH, UI_PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT)
	_panel.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_panel_style(_panel, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER, 1, 16, 10, 0.22)
	_ui_root.add_child(_panel)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 12)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 12)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 12)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 12)
	_panel.add_child(margin)

	var layout := VBoxContainer.new()
	layout.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
	margin.add_child(layout)

	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = "Debug World"
	title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 20)
	title.add_theme_color_override("font_color", PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	layout.add_child(title)

	var subtitle := Label.new()
	subtitle.text = "Checkerboard showroom for scale, collision, mount, and support-surface debugging."
	subtitle.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	subtitle.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	subtitle.add_theme_color_override("font_color", PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT_MUTED)
	layout.add_child(subtitle)

	_return_button = Button.new()
	_return_button.text = "Return To Room"
	_return_button.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, 36.0)
	_return_button.pressed.connect(_on_return_button_pressed)
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(_return_button, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_DARK, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	layout.add_child(_return_button)

	_status_label = Label.new()
	_status_label.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	_status_label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	_status_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT_MUTED)
	layout.add_child(_status_label)

	var navigation_row := HBoxContainer.new()
	navigation_row.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
	layout.add_child(navigation_row)

	_previous_item_button = Button.new()
	_previous_item_button.text = "Prev"
	_previous_item_button.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_previous_item_button.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, 34.0)
	_previous_item_button.pressed.connect(_on_previous_item_button_pressed)
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(_previous_item_button, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	navigation_row.add_child(_previous_item_button)

	_item_index_label = Label.new()
	_item_index_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(72.0, 0.0)
	_item_index_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	_item_index_label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	_item_index_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	navigation_row.add_child(_item_index_label)

	_next_item_button = Button.new()
	_next_item_button.text = "Next"
	_next_item_button.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_next_item_button.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, 34.0)
	_next_item_button.pressed.connect(_on_next_item_button_pressed)
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(_next_item_button, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	navigation_row.add_child(_next_item_button)

	_free_camera_button = Button.new()
	_free_camera_button.toggle_mode = true
	_free_camera_button.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, 34.0)
	_free_camera_button.toggled.connect(_on_free_camera_toggled)
	layout.add_child(_free_camera_button)
	_apply_free_camera_button_visual()

	_search_input = LineEdit.new()
	_search_input.placeholder_text = "Search item name..."
	_search_input.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, FILTER_ROW_HEIGHT)
	_search_input.text_changed.connect(_on_search_text_changed)
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_line_edit_style(_search_input)
	layout.add_child(_search_input)

	var filter_row := HBoxContainer.new()
	filter_row.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
	layout.add_child(filter_row)

	_mount_filter_option = OptionButton.new()
	_mount_filter_option.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, FILTER_ROW_HEIGHT)
	_mount_filter_option.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_mount_filter_option.item_selected.connect(_on_mount_filter_selected)
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(_mount_filter_option, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	filter_row.add_child(_mount_filter_option)

	_category_filter_option = OptionButton.new()
	_category_filter_option.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, FILTER_ROW_HEIGHT)
	_category_filter_option.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_category_filter_option.item_selected.connect(_on_category_filter_selected)
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(_category_filter_option, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER, PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	filter_row.add_child(_category_filter_option)

	var overlay_grid := GridContainer.new()

	overlay_grid.add_theme_constant_override("h_separation", 8)
	overlay_grid.add_theme_constant_override("v_separation", 8)
	layout.add_child(overlay_grid)

	_collision_toggle_button = _build_overlay_toggle_button("Collision", _show_collision_boxes, Callable(self, "_on_collision_toggle_pressed"))
	overlay_grid.add_child(_collision_toggle_button)
	_footprint_toggle_button = _build_overlay_toggle_button("Footprint", _show_footprints, Callable(self, "_on_footprint_toggle_pressed"))
	overlay_grid.add_child(_footprint_toggle_button)
	_support_toggle_button = _build_overlay_toggle_button("Support", _show_support_surfaces, Callable(self, "_on_support_toggle_pressed"))
	overlay_grid.add_child(_support_toggle_button)
	_anchor_toggle_button = _build_overlay_toggle_button("Anchors", _show_anchor_markers, Callable(self, "_on_anchor_toggle_pressed"))
	overlay_grid.add_child(_anchor_toggle_button)
	_label_toggle_button = _build_overlay_toggle_button("Labels", _show_labels, Callable(self, "_on_label_toggle_pressed"))
	_label_toggle_button.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	layout.add_child(_label_toggle_button)

	var selection_title := Label.new()
	selection_title.text = "Selection"
	selection_title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 14)
	selection_title.add_theme_color_override("font_color", PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT)
	layout.add_child(selection_title)

	_selection_label = Label.new()
	_selection_label.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	_selection_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(UI_PANEL_WIDTH - 48.0, 170.0)
	_selection_label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT_MUTED)
	layout.add_child(_selection_label)

	_update_status_text(0, _item_defs.size())
	_update_selection_text()

func _build_overlay_toggle_button(text_value: String, pressed_value: bool, callback: Callable) -> Button:
	var button := Button.new()
	button.toggle_mode = true
	button.button_pressed = pressed_value
	button.text = text_value
	button.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0.0, OVERLAY_BUTTON_HEIGHT)
	button.toggled.connect(callback)
	_apply_overlay_toggle_style(button, pressed_value)
	return button

func _populate_filter_options() -> void:
	if _mount_filter_option == null or _category_filter_option == null:
		return

	_mount_filter_option.clear()
	_mount_filter_option.add_item("Type: All")
	_mount_filter_option.set_item_metadata(0, "")
	_mount_filter_option.add_item("Type: Floor")
	_mount_filter_option.set_item_metadata(1, RoomConstants.MOUNT_FLOOR)
	_mount_filter_option.add_item("Type: Wall")
	_mount_filter_option.set_item_metadata(2, RoomConstants.MOUNT_WALL)
	_mount_filter_option.add_item("Type: Ceiling")
	_mount_filter_option.set_item_metadata(3, RoomConstants.MOUNT_CEILING)
	_mount_filter_option.add_item("Type: Surface")
	_mount_filter_option.set_item_metadata(4, RoomConstants.MOUNT_SURFACE)
	_mount_filter_option.select(0)

	_category_filter_option.clear()
	_category_filter_option.add_item("Category: All")
	_category_filter_option.set_item_metadata(0, "")
	var categories := PlacementInventoryCatalog.build_category_names(_item_defs)
	for category_name in categories:
		_category_filter_option.add_item("Category: %s" % category_name)
		_category_filter_option.set_item_metadata(_category_filter_option.item_count - 1, category_name)
	_category_filter_option.select(0)

func _set_showroom_visible(visible_state: bool) -> void:
	if _showroom_root != null:
		_showroom_root.visible = visible_state
	if _ui_layer != null:
		_ui_layer.visible = visible_state

func _apply_external_mode_state(debug_enabled: bool) -> void:
	if _room_shell != null:
		_room_shell.visible = not debug_enabled
	if _placement_manager != null:
		_placement_manager.set_debug_world_active(debug_enabled)
	if _player != null and _player.has_method("set_debug_world_active"):
		_player.call("set_debug_world_active", debug_enabled)
	_set_room_camera_current(not debug_enabled)

func _focus_showroom_camera() -> void:
	if _selected_item != null:
		_focus_item_view_camera(_selected_item)
		return
	_focus_overview_camera()

func _restore_room_camera() -> void:
	if _item_view_camera != null:
		_item_view_camera.current = false
	if _free_camera != null:
		_free_camera.current = false
	if _room_camera_controller != null and _has_saved_camera_target:
		_room_camera_controller.target_position = _saved_camera_target
		_room_camera_controller.reset_camera()
	_set_room_camera_current(true)

func _set_room_camera_current(current_value: bool) -> void:
	var target_camera: Camera3D = null
	if _room_camera_controller != null and _room_camera_controller.has_method("get_camera"):
		target_camera = _room_camera_controller.get_camera()
	elif _player != null and _player.has_method("get_active_camera"):
		target_camera = _player.call("get_active_camera") as Camera3D
		
	if target_camera != null:
		target_camera.current = current_value

func _focus_overview_camera() -> void:
	if _item_view_camera == null:
		return
	var look_target := global_position + SHOWROOM_CAMERA_TARGET_LOCAL
	var direction := ITEM_VIEW_CAMERA_DIRECTION.normalized()
	var distance := 26.0
	_item_view_camera.size = 18.0
	_item_view_camera.global_position = look_target + direction * distance
	_item_view_camera.look_at(look_target, Vector3.UP)
	_item_view_camera.current = true
	if _free_camera != null:
		_free_camera.current = false

func _focus_item_view_camera(item: SimpleWoodChair) -> void:
	if _item_view_camera == null or item == null:
		return
	var look_target := item.global_position + item.get_collision_center_offset()
	var collision_size := item.get_collision_size()
	var max_dimension := maxf(collision_size.x, maxf(collision_size.y, collision_size.z))
	var direction := ITEM_VIEW_CAMERA_DIRECTION.normalized()
	var distance := maxf(ITEM_VIEW_CAMERA_MIN_DISTANCE, max_dimension * 3.8)
	_item_view_camera.size = maxf(ITEM_VIEW_CAMERA_MIN_SIZE, max_dimension * ITEM_VIEW_CAMERA_PADDING)
	_item_view_camera.global_position = look_target + direction * distance
	_item_view_camera.look_at(look_target, Vector3.UP)
	_item_view_camera.current = true
	if _free_camera != null:
		_free_camera.current = false

func _set_free_camera_active(active: bool) -> void:
	_free_camera_active = active
	_free_camera_looking = false
	if _free_camera_button != null:
		_free_camera_button.set_pressed_no_signal(active)
	_apply_free_camera_button_visual()
	if active:
		if _selected_item != null and _item_view_camera != null and _free_camera != null:
			_free_camera.global_transform = _item_view_camera.global_transform
			var euler := _free_camera.rotation
			_free_camera_pitch = euler.x
			_free_camera_yaw = euler.y
			_sync_free_camera_rotation()
		if _free_camera != null:
			_free_camera.current = true
		if _item_view_camera != null:
			_item_view_camera.current = false
	else:
		if _selected_item != null:
			_focus_item_view_camera(_selected_item)
		else:
			_focus_overview_camera()
	_update_status_text(_item_records.size(), _item_defs.size())

func _sync_free_camera_rotation() -> void:
	if _free_camera == null:
		return
	_free_camera.rotation = Vector3(_free_camera_pitch, _free_camera_yaw, 0.0)

func _rebuild_showroom_items() -> void:
	if _free_camera_active:
		_set_free_camera_active(false)
	_clear_showroom_items()
	_showroom_dirty = false

	var filtered_defs := _get_filtered_item_defs()
	var floor_defs: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var wall_defs: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var ceiling_defs: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var surface_defs: Array[Dictionary] = []

	for item_def in filtered_defs:
		match PlacementInventoryCatalog.get_primary_mount_kind(item_def):
			RoomConstants.MOUNT_WALL:
				wall_defs.append(item_def)
			RoomConstants.MOUNT_CEILING:
				ceiling_defs.append(item_def)
			RoomConstants.MOUNT_SURFACE:
				surface_defs.append(item_def)
			_:
				floor_defs.append(item_def)

	_layout_floor_items(floor_defs)
	_layout_wall_items(wall_defs)
	_layout_ceiling_items(ceiling_defs)
	_layout_surface_items(surface_defs)
	if not _item_records.is_empty():
		_set_selected_item(_item_records[0].get("item", null) as SimpleWoodChair)
	else:
		_set_selected_item(null)
	_update_overlay_visibility()
	_update_status_text(filtered_defs.size(), _item_defs.size())
	_update_selection_text()
	_update_navigation_ui()

func _clear_showroom_items() -> void:
	for child in _items_root.get_children():
		_items_root.remove_child(child)
		child.queue_free()
	for child in _dynamic_geometry_root.get_children():
		_dynamic_geometry_root.remove_child(child)
		child.queue_free()
	_item_records.clear()
	_item_record_lookup.clear()
	_selected_item = null
	_selected_item_index = -1

func _layout_floor_items(item_defs: Array[Dictionary]) -> void:
	if item_defs.is_empty():
		return

	var columns := clampi(int(ceil(sqrt(float(item_defs.size())))), 4, 9)
	var row_count := int(ceil(float(item_defs.size()) / float(columns)))
	var start_x := -((float(columns) - 1.0) * FLOOR_CELL_SPACING.x) * 0.5
	var start_z := FLOOR_SECTION_CENTER_Z - ((float(row_count) - 1.0) * FLOOR_CELL_SPACING.y) * 0.5

	for index in item_defs.size():
		var item_def := item_defs[index]
		var column := index % columns
		var row := index / columns
		var item_position := Vector3(
			start_x + float(column) * FLOOR_CELL_SPACING.x + 0.5,
			0.0,
			start_z + float(row) * FLOOR_CELL_SPACING.y + 0.5
		)
		_spawn_debug_item(item_def, item_position, 0.0)

func _layout_wall_items(item_defs: Array[Dictionary]) -> void:
	if item_defs.is_empty():
		return

	var columns := clampi(int(ceil(float(item_defs.size()) / 2.0)), 4, 11)
	var rows := int(ceil(float(item_defs.size()) / float(columns)))
	var start_x := -((float(columns) - 1.0) * WALL_HORIZONTAL_SPACING) * 0.5
	var start_y := WALL_ITEM_BASE_Y + maxf(0.0, float(rows - 1)) * -WALL_VERTICAL_SPACING * 0.5

	for index in item_defs.size():
		var item_def := item_defs[index]
		var placeable := _create_item_instance(item_def)
		if placeable == null:
			continue
		var column := index % columns
		var row := index / columns
		var x_value := start_x + float(column) * WALL_HORIZONTAL_SPACING + 0.5
		var y_value := start_y + float(row) * WALL_VERTICAL_SPACING
		var placed_position := Vector3(x_value, y_value, WALL_SECTION_SURFACE_Z)
		if not placeable.requires_wall_opening():
			placed_position += PlacementSurfaceQueries.get_wall_interior_normal(RoomConstants.WALL_BACK) * placeable.get_wall_mount_depth_offset()
		_finalize_spawned_item(placeable, item_def, placed_position, RoomConstants.get_wall_rotation(RoomConstants.WALL_BACK) + placeable.get_wall_rotation_offset())

func _layout_ceiling_items(item_defs: Array[Dictionary]) -> void:
	if item_defs.is_empty():
		return

	var columns := clampi(int(ceil(sqrt(float(item_defs.size())))), 2, 5)
	var rows := int(ceil(float(item_defs.size()) / float(columns)))
	var start_x := -((float(columns) - 1.0) * CEILING_CELL_SPACING.x) * 0.5
	var start_z := CEILING_SECTION_CENTER_Z - ((float(rows) - 1.0) * CEILING_CELL_SPACING.y) * 0.5

	for index in item_defs.size():
		var item_def := item_defs[index]
		var column := index % columns
		var row := index / columns
		var item_position := Vector3(
			start_x + float(column) * CEILING_CELL_SPACING.x + 0.5,
			CEILING_SURFACE_Y,
			start_z + float(row) * CEILING_CELL_SPACING.y + 0.5
		)
		_spawn_debug_item(item_def, item_position, 0.0)

func _layout_surface_items(item_defs: Array[Dictionary]) -> void:
	if item_defs.is_empty():
		return

	var columns := clampi(int(ceil(sqrt(float(item_defs.size())))), 3, 7)
	var rows := int(ceil(float(item_defs.size()) / float(columns)))
	var start_x := -((float(columns) - 1.0) * SURFACE_CELL_SPACING.x) * 0.5
	var start_z := SURFACE_SECTION_CENTER_Z - ((float(rows) - 1.0) * SURFACE_CELL_SPACING.y) * 0.5

	for index in item_defs.size():
		var item_def := item_defs[index]
		var column := index % columns
		var row := index / columns
		var stand_center := Vector3(
			start_x + float(column) * SURFACE_CELL_SPACING.x + 0.5,
			0.0,
			start_z + float(row) * SURFACE_CELL_SPACING.y + 0.5
		)
		_dynamic_geometry_root.add_child(_build_surface_stand(stand_center))
		var item_position := Vector3(
			stand_center.x,
			SURFACE_STAND_HEIGHT + SURFACE_STAND_TOP_THICKNESS + SURFACE_ITEM_CLEARANCE,
			stand_center.z
		)
		_spawn_debug_item(item_def, item_position, 0.0)

func _build_surface_stand(local_center: Vector3) -> Node3D:
	var stand_root := Node3D.new()
	stand_root.position = local_center

	var column := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var column_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	column_mesh.size = Vector3(0.34, SURFACE_STAND_HEIGHT, 0.34)
	column.mesh = column_mesh
	column.position.y = SURFACE_STAND_HEIGHT * 0.5
	column.material_override = _make_solid_material(Color(0.47, 0.33, 0.2, 1.0), 0.96)
	stand_root.add_child(column)

	var top := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var top_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	top_mesh.size = Vector3(
		SURFACE_STAND_HALF_EXTENTS.x * 2.0,
		SURFACE_STAND_TOP_THICKNESS,
		SURFACE_STAND_HALF_EXTENTS.y * 2.0
	)
	top.mesh = top_mesh
	top.position.y = SURFACE_STAND_HEIGHT + SURFACE_STAND_TOP_THICKNESS * 0.5
	top.material_override = _make_solid_material(Color(0.67, 0.52, 0.34, 1.0), 0.96)
	stand_root.add_child(top)

	var support_overlay := _build_flat_overlay(
		Vector2(SURFACE_STAND_HALF_EXTENTS.x * 2.0 - 0.1, SURFACE_STAND_HALF_EXTENTS.y * 2.0 - 0.1),
		Vector3(0.0, SURFACE_STAND_HEIGHT + SURFACE_STAND_TOP_THICKNESS + SUPPORT_SURFACE_THICKNESS * 0.5, 0.0),
		Color(0.34, 0.82, 0.62, 0.12),
		SUPPORT_SURFACE_THICKNESS
	)
	support_overlay.visible = _show_support_surfaces
	stand_root.add_child(support_overlay)
	return stand_root

func _spawn_debug_item(item_def: Dictionary, local_position: Vector3, rotation_y: float) -> void:
	var placeable := _create_item_instance(item_def)
	if placeable == null:
		return
	_finalize_spawned_item(placeable, item_def, local_position, rotation_y)

func _finalize_spawned_item(placeable: SimpleWoodChair, item_def: Dictionary, local_position: Vector3, rotation_y: float) -> void:
	placeable.position = local_position
	placeable.rotation = Vector3(0.0, rotation_y, 0.0)
	placeable.ensure_runtime_visual_setup()
	_items_root.add_child(placeable)
	_register_item_record(placeable, item_def)

func _create_item_instance(item_def: Dictionary) -> SimpleWoodChair:
	if PlacementInventoryCatalog.uses_imported_scene_factory(item_def):
		return PlacementInventoryCatalog.create_imported_scene_instance(item_def) as SimpleWoodChair
	var script_ref := PlacementInventoryCatalog.get_item_script(item_def)
	if script_ref == null:
		return null
	return script_ref.new() as SimpleWoodChair

func _register_item_record(placeable: SimpleWoodChair, item_def: Dictionary) -> void:
	if placeable == null:
		return

	var label := _build_item_label(placeable, item_def)
	placeable.add_child(label)

	var collision_overlay := _build_collision_overlay(placeable)
	placeable.add_child(collision_overlay)

	var anchor_overlay := _build_anchor_overlay()
	placeable.add_child(anchor_overlay)

	var footprint_overlays: Array = _build_footprint_overlays(placeable)
	for overlay in footprint_overlays:
		placeable.add_child(overlay)

	var support_overlays: Array = _build_support_surface_overlays(placeable)
	for overlay in support_overlays:
		placeable.add_child(overlay)

	var record := {
		"item": placeable,
		"item_def": item_def.duplicate(true),
		"label": label,
		"collision": collision_overlay,
		"anchor": anchor_overlay,
		"footprints": footprint_overlays,
		"supports": support_overlays,
	}
	_item_records.append(record)
	_item_record_lookup[placeable.get_instance_id()] = record

func _build_item_label(placeable: SimpleWoodChair, item_def: Dictionary) -> Label3D:
	var mount_label := PlacementInventoryCatalog.get_mount_badge_text(item_def)
	var label := Label3D.new()
	label.text = "%s\n%s" % [placeable.get_display_name(), mount_label]
	label.position = Vector3(0.0, _get_label_height(placeable), 0.0)
	label.font_size = 22
	label.pixel_size = 0.01
	label.modulate = PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT
	label.billboard = LABEL_BILLBOARD
	label.outline_size = 8
	return label

func _build_collision_overlay(placeable: SimpleWoodChair) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mesh_instance := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var box_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	box_mesh.size = placeable.get_collision_size()
	mesh_instance.mesh = box_mesh
	mesh_instance.position = placeable.get_collision_center_offset()
	mesh_instance.material_override = _make_overlay_material(Color(0.36, 0.88, 1.0, 0.13))
	mesh_instance.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	return mesh_instance

func _build_anchor_overlay() -> MeshInstance3D:
	var anchor := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var sphere_mesh := SphereMesh.new()
	sphere_mesh.radius = ANCHOR_MARKER_RADIUS
	sphere_mesh.height = ANCHOR_MARKER_RADIUS * 2.0
	anchor.mesh = sphere_mesh
	anchor.material_override = _make_overlay_material(Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.38, 0.38))
	anchor.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	return anchor

func _build_footprint_overlays(placeable: SimpleWoodChair) -> Array:
	var overlays: Array = []
	var mount_kind := placeable.get_primary_mount_kind()
	if mount_kind == RoomConstants.MOUNT_WALL:
		var wall_half_extents := placeable.get_wall_half_extents()
		if wall_half_extents.length_squared() > 0.0001:
			var wall_overlay := MeshInstance3D.new()
			var wall_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
			wall_mesh.size = Vector3(
				wall_half_extents.x * 2.0,
				wall_half_extents.y * 2.0,
				WALL_EXTENTS_THICKNESS
			)
			wall_overlay.mesh = wall_mesh
			wall_overlay.position = Vector3(
				0.0,
				placeable.get_collision_center_offset().y,
				-placeable.get_wall_mount_depth_offset()
			)
			wall_overlay.material_override = _make_overlay_material(Color(0.96, 0.7, 0.32, 0.14))
			wall_overlay.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
			overlays.append(wall_overlay)
		return overlays

	var footprint_half_extents := placeable.get_footprint_half_extents()
	if footprint_half_extents.length_squared() <= 0.0001:
		return overlays
	var footprint_overlay := _build_flat_overlay(
		Vector2(footprint_half_extents.x * 2.0, footprint_half_extents.y * 2.0),
		Vector3(0.0, FOOTPRINT_THICKNESS * 0.5, 0.0),
		Color(0.96, 0.7, 0.32, 0.13),
		FOOTPRINT_THICKNESS
	)
	overlays.append(footprint_overlay)
	return overlays

func _build_support_surface_overlays(placeable: SimpleWoodChair) -> Array:
	var overlays: Array = []
	for support_surface in placeable.get_support_surfaces():
		var center_offset := support_surface.get("center_offset", Vector3.ZERO) as Vector3
		var half_extents := support_surface.get("half_extents", Vector2.ZERO) as Vector2
		if half_extents.x <= 0.001 or half_extents.y <= 0.001:
			continue
		var overlay := _build_flat_overlay(
			Vector2(half_extents.x * 2.0, half_extents.y * 2.0),
			center_offset + Vector3(0.0, SUPPORT_SURFACE_THICKNESS * 0.5, 0.0),
			Color(0.34, 0.82, 0.62, 0.14),
			SUPPORT_SURFACE_THICKNESS
		)
		overlays.append(overlay)
	return overlays

func _build_flat_overlay(size: Vector2, local_position: Vector3, color_value: Color, thickness: float) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var overlay := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var box_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	box_mesh.size = Vector3(size.x, thickness, size.y)
	overlay.mesh = box_mesh
	overlay.position = local_position
	overlay.material_override = _make_overlay_material(color_value)
	overlay.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	return overlay

func _make_overlay_material(color_value: Color) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var material := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	material.albedo_color = color_value
	material.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	material.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	material.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED
	material.no_depth_test = false
	material.emission_enabled = true
	material.emission = color_value
	return material

func _make_solid_material(color_value: Color, roughness_value: float) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var material := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	material.albedo_color = color_value
	material.roughness = roughness_value
	material.metallic = 0.0
	return material

func _get_label_height(placeable: SimpleWoodChair) -> float:
	var collision_size := placeable.get_collision_size()
	var collision_center := placeable.get_collision_center_offset()
	var local_top := collision_center.y + collision_size.y * 0.5
	return maxf(local_top + LABEL_LIFT, 0.72)

func _get_filtered_item_defs() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var filtered: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var search_value := _search_text.strip_edges().to_lower()
	for item_def in _item_defs:
		var mount_kind := PlacementInventoryCatalog.get_primary_mount_kind(item_def)
		if not _selected_mount_kind.is_empty() and mount_kind != _selected_mount_kind:
			continue
		var category_name := String(item_def.get("category", "Miscellaneous"))
		if not _selected_category.is_empty() and category_name != _selected_category:
			continue
		if not search_value.is_empty():
			var display_name := String(item_def.get("display_name", "")).to_lower()
			var item_id := String(item_def.get("id", "")).to_lower()
			if not display_name.contains(search_value) and not item_id.contains(search_value):
				continue
		filtered.append(item_def)
	return filtered

func _pick_item_from_mouse(mouse_position: Vector2) -> SimpleWoodChair:
	var active_camera := _get_active_debug_camera()
	if active_camera == null:
		return null
	var space_state := get_world_3d().direct_space_state
	var hit := PlacementSurfaceQueries.raycast_from_mouse(
		space_state,
		active_camera,
		mouse_position,
		SimpleWoodChair.COLLISION_LAYER
	)
	if hit.is_empty():
		return null
	var collider: Variant = hit.get("collider", null)
	if collider is SimpleWoodChair and _item_record_lookup.has((collider as SimpleWoodChair).get_instance_id()):
		return collider as SimpleWoodChair
	return null

func _is_pointer_over_ui() -> bool:
	return get_viewport().gui_get_hovered_control() != null

func _set_selected_item(item: SimpleWoodChair) -> void:
	_selected_item = item
	_selected_item_index = _find_item_index(item)
	if _debug_world_enabled and not _free_camera_active:
		if _selected_item != null:
			_focus_item_view_camera(_selected_item)
		else:
			_focus_overview_camera()
	_update_record_visual_states()
	_update_selection_text()
	_update_navigation_ui()

func _update_record_visual_states() -> void:
	for record in _item_records:
		var placeable := record.get("item", null) as SimpleWoodChair
		var label := record.get("label", null) as Label3D
		var collision_overlay := record.get("collision", null) as MeshInstance3D
		var anchor_overlay := record.get("anchor", null) as MeshInstance3D
		var is_selected := placeable != null and placeable == _selected_item
		if label != null:
			label.modulate = PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT if is_selected else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT
		_set_overlay_color(collision_overlay, Color(1.0, 0.52, 0.36, 0.16) if is_selected else Color(0.36, 0.88, 1.0, 0.13))
		_set_overlay_color(anchor_overlay, Color(1.0, 0.74, 0.34, 0.48) if is_selected else Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.38, 0.38))

func _set_overlay_color(mesh_instance: MeshInstance3D, color_value: Color) -> void:
	if mesh_instance == null:
		return
	var material := mesh_instance.material_override as StandardMaterial3D
	if material == null:
		return
	material.albedo_color = color_value
	material.emission = color_value

func _get_active_debug_camera() -> Camera3D:
	if _free_camera_active and _free_camera != null:
		return _free_camera
	return _item_view_camera

func _find_item_index(item: SimpleWoodChair) -> int:
	if item == null:
		return -1
	for index in _item_records.size():
		var placeable := _item_records[index].get("item", null) as SimpleWoodChair
		if placeable == item:
			return index
	return -1

func _focus_relative_item(direction: int) -> void:
	if _item_records.is_empty():
		return
	var next_index := _selected_item_index
	if next_index < 0:
		next_index = 0
	else:
		next_index = wrapi(next_index + direction, 0, _item_records.size())
	_set_free_camera_active(false)
	_set_selected_item(_item_records[next_index].get("item", null) as SimpleWoodChair)

func _update_navigation_ui() -> void:
	if _item_index_label != null:
		_item_index_label.text = "%d / %d" % [maxi(_selected_item_index + 1, 0), _item_records.size()]
	if _previous_item_button != null:
		_previous_item_button.disabled = _item_records.size() <= 1
	if _next_item_button != null:
		_next_item_button.disabled = _item_records.size() <= 1

func _apply_free_camera_button_visual() -> void:
	if _free_camera_button == null:
		return
	_free_camera_button.text = "Free Camera [On]" if _free_camera_active else "Free Camera [Off]"
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(
		_free_camera_button,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_DARK if _free_camera_active else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT if _free_camera_active else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT
	)

func _update_overlay_visibility() -> void:
	for record in _item_records:
		var label := record.get("label", null) as Label3D
		var collision_overlay := record.get("collision", null) as MeshInstance3D
		var anchor_overlay := record.get("anchor", null) as MeshInstance3D
		if label != null:
			label.visible = _show_labels
		if collision_overlay != null:
			collision_overlay.visible = _show_collision_boxes
		if anchor_overlay != null:
			anchor_overlay.visible = _show_anchor_markers
		for overlay in record.get("footprints", []):
			if overlay is MeshInstance3D:
				(overlay as MeshInstance3D).visible = _show_footprints
		for overlay in record.get("supports", []):
			if overlay is MeshInstance3D:
				(overlay as MeshInstance3D).visible = _show_support_surfaces
	_update_record_visual_states()

func _update_status_text(filtered_count: int, total_count: int) -> void:
	if _status_label == null:
		return
	var mode_text := "Free Camera: RMB look, WASD move, Q/E rise, wheel changes speed." if _free_camera_active else "Item View: use Prev/Next or click an item to inspect it."
	_status_label.text = "%d shown of %d catalog items. %s" % [filtered_count, total_count, mode_text]

func _update_selection_text() -> void:
	if _selection_label == null:
		return
	if _selected_item == null:
		_selection_label.text = "No item selected.\n\nLeft-click an item to inspect its current collision size, anchor offset, footprint, support surfaces, and source asset."
		return
	var record := _item_record_lookup.get(_selected_item.get_instance_id(), {}) as Dictionary
	var item_def := record.get("item_def", {}) as Dictionary
	var category_name := String(item_def.get("category", "Miscellaneous"))
	var mount_kind := PlacementInventoryCatalog.get_primary_mount_kind(item_def)
	var source_path := String(item_def.get("source_scene_path", ""))
	if source_path.is_empty():
		var script_ref := PlacementInventoryCatalog.get_item_script(item_def)
		source_path = script_ref.resource_path if script_ref != null else "Built-in"
	var support_surface_count := _selected_item.get_support_surfaces().size()
	_selection_label.text = (
		"Name: %s\nCategory: %s\nMount: %s\nCollision: %s\nCenter Offset: %s\nFootprint: %s\nWall Extents: %s\nSupport Surfaces: %d\nSource: %s"
		% [
			_selected_item.get_display_name(),
			category_name,
			mount_kind,
			_selected_item.get_collision_size(),
			_selected_item.get_collision_center_offset(),
			_selected_item.get_footprint_half_extents(),
			_selected_item.get_wall_half_extents(),
			support_surface_count,
			source_path,
		]
	)

func _apply_overlay_toggle_style(button: Button, enabled: bool) -> void:
	if button == null:
		return
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(
		button,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_DARK if enabled else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT if enabled else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT
	)

func _on_return_button_pressed() -> void:
	set_debug_world_enabled(false)

func _on_search_text_changed(next_text: String) -> void:
	_search_text = next_text
	_rebuild_showroom_items()

func _on_previous_item_button_pressed() -> void:
	_focus_relative_item(-1)

func _on_next_item_button_pressed() -> void:
	_focus_relative_item(1)

func _on_free_camera_toggled(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_set_free_camera_active(enabled)

func _on_mount_filter_selected(index: int) -> void:
	_selected_mount_kind = String(_mount_filter_option.get_item_metadata(index))
	_rebuild_showroom_items()

func _on_category_filter_selected(index: int) -> void:
	_selected_category = String(_category_filter_option.get_item_metadata(index))
	_rebuild_showroom_items()

func _on_collision_toggle_pressed(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_show_collision_boxes = enabled
	_apply_overlay_toggle_style(_collision_toggle_button, enabled)
	_update_overlay_visibility()

func _on_footprint_toggle_pressed(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_show_footprints = enabled
	_apply_overlay_toggle_style(_footprint_toggle_button, enabled)
	_update_overlay_visibility()

func _on_support_toggle_pressed(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_show_support_surfaces = enabled
	_apply_overlay_toggle_style(_support_toggle_button, enabled)
	_update_overlay_visibility()

func _on_anchor_toggle_pressed(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_show_anchor_markers = enabled
	_apply_overlay_toggle_style(_anchor_toggle_button, enabled)
	_update_overlay_visibility()

func _on_label_toggle_pressed(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_show_labels = enabled
	_apply_overlay_toggle_style(_label_toggle_button, enabled)
	_update_overlay_visibility()

class_name DeveloperEnvironmentPanel
extends CanvasLayer

signal environment_state_changed

const PANEL_HEIGHT := 620.0

const BUTTON_HEIGHT := 36.0
const PERSISTENT_SETTINGS_PATH := DeveloperEnvironmentPersistence.SETTINGS_PATH
const PERSISTENT_SAVE_DELAY_SECONDS := 0.15
const EDITOR_PREVIEW_POLL_SECONDS := 0.6
const SUN_PRESET_MORNING := DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.SUN_PRESET_MORNING
const SUN_PRESET_NOON := DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.SUN_PRESET_NOON
const SUN_PRESET_SUNSET := DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.SUN_PRESET_SUNSET
const SUN_PRESET_AFTERNOON_COZY := DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.SUN_PRESET_AFTERNOON_COZY

@export var world_environment_path: NodePath
@export var directional_light_path: NodePath
@export var debug_world_controller_path: NodePath

var _world_environment: WorldEnvironment
var _directional_light: DirectionalLight3D
var _environment: Environment
var _debug_world_controller: DebugWorldController

var _default_values: Dictionary = {}
var _base_ambient_color := Color.WHITE
var _base_fog_color := Color.WHITE
var _base_light_color := Color.WHITE
var _warmth := 0.0
var _syncing_controls := false

var _ui_root: Control
var _toggle_button: Button
var _panel: PanelContainer
var _debug_world_button: Button
var _save_timer: Timer
var _status_label: Label
var _slider_bindings: Array[Dictionary] = []
var _toggle_bindings: Array[Dictionary] = []
var _editor_preview_poll_time := 0.0
var _editor_persistent_signature := ""
var _syncing_debug_world_button := false

func _ready() -> void:
	layer = 20
	_world_environment = get_node_or_null(world_environment_path) as WorldEnvironment
	_directional_light = get_node_or_null(directional_light_path) as DirectionalLight3D
	_environment = _world_environment.environment if _world_environment != null else null
	_debug_world_controller = get_node_or_null(debug_world_controller_path) as DebugWorldController
	var debug_world_callback := Callable(self, "_on_debug_world_enabled_changed")
	if _debug_world_controller != null and not _debug_world_controller.is_connected("debug_world_enabled_changed", debug_world_callback):
		_debug_world_controller.connect("debug_world_enabled_changed", debug_world_callback)

	_capture_defaults()
	if Engine.is_editor_hint():
		_refresh_editor_preview_from_saved_state(true)
		set_process(true)
		return

	_build_ui()
	var loaded_saved_settings := _load_persistent_state()
	_sync_controls_from_state()
	_set_status("Loaded saved developer preset from user://" if loaded_saved_settings else "Using scene defaults. Changes auto-save to user://")

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not Engine.is_editor_hint():
		return

	_editor_preview_poll_time -= delta
	if _editor_preview_poll_time > 0.0:
		return

	_editor_preview_poll_time = EDITOR_PREVIEW_POLL_SECONDS
	_refresh_editor_preview_from_saved_state()

func _capture_defaults() -> void:
	var captured := DeveloperEnvironmentState.capture_defaults(_environment, _directional_light)
	_default_values = captured.get("values", {}) as Dictionary
	_base_ambient_color = captured.get("base_ambient_color", Color.WHITE) as Color
	_base_fog_color = captured.get("base_fog_color", Color.WHITE) as Color
	_base_light_color = captured.get("base_light_color", Color.WHITE) as Color

func _add_section_label(parent: VBoxContainer, text_value: String) -> void:
	var separator := HSeparator.new()

func _add_sun_preset_controls(parent: VBoxContainer) -> void:
	var label := Label.new()
	label.text = "Sun Presets"
	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	parent.add_child(label)

	var grid := GridContainer.new()
	grid.columns = 2
	grid.add_theme_constant_override("h_separation", 8)
	grid.add_theme_constant_override("v_separation", 8)
	parent.add_child(grid)

	for preset_name in DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.get_preset_names():
		var button := Button.new()
		button.text = _get_sun_preset_label(preset_name)
		button.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
		button.pressed.connect(_on_sun_preset_pressed.bind(preset_name))
		grid.add_child(button)

func _get_sun_preset_label(preset_name: String) -> String:
	return DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.get_label(preset_name)

func _add_slider_control(parent: VBoxContainer, title_text: String, min_value: float, max_value: float, step_value: float, getter: Callable, setter: Callable) -> void:
	var wrapper := VBoxContainer.new()
	wrapper.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	parent.add_child(wrapper)

	var header := HBoxContainer.new()
	wrapper.add_child(header)

	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = title_text
	title.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	header.add_child(title)

	var value_label := Label.new()
	value_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(56.0, 0.0)
	value_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	header.add_child(value_label)

	var slider := HSlider.new()
	slider.min_value = min_value
	slider.max_value = max_value
	slider.step = step_value
	slider.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	slider.value_changed.connect(_on_slider_value_changed.bind(setter, value_label, step_value))
	wrapper.add_child(slider)

	_slider_bindings.append({
		"slider": slider,
		"value_label": value_label,
		"getter": getter,
		"step": step_value,
	})

func _add_toggle_control(parent: VBoxContainer, title_text: String, getter: Callable, setter: Callable) -> void:
	var toggle := CheckButton.new()
	toggle.text = title_text
	toggle.toggled.connect(_on_toggle_value_changed.bind(setter))
	parent.add_child(toggle)

	_toggle_bindings.append({
		"toggle": toggle,
		"getter": getter,
	})

func _on_toggle_button_pressed() -> void:
	var is_open := _toggle_button.button_pressed
	_panel.visible = is_open
	_toggle_button.text = "Developer [Hide]" if is_open else "Developer [Show]"
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(
		_toggle_button,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_DARK if is_open else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT if is_open else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT
	)

func _on_debug_world_button_pressed() -> void:
	if _syncing_debug_world_button or _debug_world_controller == null or _debug_world_button == null:
		return
	_debug_world_controller.set_debug_world_enabled(_debug_world_button.button_pressed)

func _on_debug_world_enabled_changed(enabled: bool) -> void:
	_update_debug_world_button_visual(enabled)

func _update_debug_world_button_visual(enabled: bool) -> void:
	if _debug_world_button == null:
		return
	_syncing_debug_world_button = true
	_debug_world_button.button_pressed = enabled
	_debug_world_button.text = "Debug World [Hide]" if enabled else "Debug World [Show]"
	PlacementUiStyles.apply_button_style(
		_debug_world_button,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_DARK if enabled else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_PANEL_ALT,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_ACCENT_BRIGHT if enabled else PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_BORDER,
		PlacementUiStyles.COLOR_TEXT
	)
	_syncing_debug_world_button = false

func _on_sun_preset_pressed(preset_name: String) -> void:
	_apply_sun_preset(preset_name)
	_sync_controls_from_state()
	_queue_persistent_save()
	_set_status("Applied %s sun preset. Saving locally..." % _get_sun_preset_label(preset_name))

func _on_reset_defaults_pressed() -> void:
	_apply_state_values(_default_values)
	_sync_controls_from_state()
	_queue_persistent_save()
	_set_status("Reset to scene defaults. Saving locally...")

func _on_clear_saved_pressed() -> void:
	_apply_state_values(_default_values)
	_sync_controls_from_state()
	if _save_timer != null:
		_save_timer.stop()

	var absolute_path := ProjectSettings.globalize_path(PERSISTENT_SETTINGS_PATH)
	if FileAccess.file_exists(absolute_path):
		var remove_error := DirAccess.remove_absolute(absolute_path)
		if remove_error != OK:
			_set_status("Could not remove saved preset (%s)." % error_string(remove_error))
			return

	_set_status("Cleared saved preset. Using scene defaults.")

func _on_slider_value_changed(value: float, setter: Callable, value_label: Label, step_value: float) -> void:
	value_label.text = _format_number(value, step_value)
	if _syncing_controls:
		return
	setter.call(value)

func _on_toggle_value_changed(value: bool, setter: Callable) -> void:
	if _syncing_controls:
		return
	setter.call(value)

func _sync_controls_from_state() -> void:
	_syncing_controls = true

	for binding in _slider_bindings:
		var getter: Callable = binding.get("getter", Callable())
		var slider: HSlider = binding.get("slider", null) as HSlider
		var value_label: Label = binding.get("value_label", null) as Label
		var step_value: float = float(binding.get("step", 0.01))
		if slider == null or value_label == null:
			continue

		var current_value: float = float(getter.call())
		slider.value = current_value
		value_label.text = _format_number(current_value, step_value)

	for binding in _toggle_bindings:
		var getter: Callable = binding.get("getter", Callable())
		var toggle: CheckButton = binding.get("toggle", null) as CheckButton
		if toggle == null:
			continue
		toggle.button_pressed = bool(getter.call())

	_syncing_controls = false

func _format_number(value: float, step_value: float) -> String:
	if step_value >= 1.0:
		return str(int(round(value)))
	if step_value >= 0.1:
		return "%.1f" % value
	if step_value >= 0.01:
		return "%.2f" % value
	return "%.3f" % value

func _get_light_energy() -> float:
	return _directional_light.light_energy if _directional_light != null else 0.0

func _set_light_energy(value: float) -> void:
	if _directional_light != null:
		_directional_light.light_energy = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_warmth() -> float:
	return _warmth

func _set_warmth(value: float) -> void:
	_warmth = value
	_apply_warmth()
	_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_shadow_enabled() -> bool:
	return _directional_light.shadow_enabled if _directional_light != null else false

func _set_shadow_enabled(value: bool) -> void:
	if _directional_light != null:
		_directional_light.shadow_enabled = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_shadow_opacity() -> float:
	return _directional_light.shadow_opacity if _directional_light != null else 0.0

func _set_shadow_opacity(value: float) -> void:
	if _directional_light != null:
		_directional_light.shadow_opacity = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_shadow_blur() -> float:
	return _directional_light.shadow_blur if _directional_light != null else 0.0

func _set_shadow_blur(value: float) -> void:
	if _directional_light != null:
		_directional_light.shadow_blur = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_shadow_distance() -> float:
	return _directional_light.directional_shadow_max_distance if _directional_light != null else 0.0

func _set_shadow_distance(value: float) -> void:
	if _directional_light != null:
		_directional_light.directional_shadow_max_distance = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_ambient_energy() -> float:
	return _environment.ambient_light_energy if _environment != null else 0.0

func _set_ambient_energy(value: float) -> void:
	if _environment != null:
		_environment.ambient_light_energy = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_ambient_sky_mix() -> float:
	return _environment.ambient_light_sky_contribution if _environment != null else 0.0

func _set_ambient_sky_mix(value: float) -> void:
	if _environment != null:
		_environment.ambient_light_sky_contribution = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_exposure() -> float:
	return _environment.tonemap_exposure if _environment != null else 0.0

func _set_exposure(value: float) -> void:
	if _environment != null:
		_environment.tonemap_exposure = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _get_post_adjust_enabled() -> bool:
	return _environment.adjustment_enabled if _environment != null else false

func _set_post_adjust_enabled(value: bool) -> void:
	if _environment != null:
		_environment.adjustment_enabled = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _set_brightness(value: float) -> void:

		_environment.adjustment_enabled = true
		_environment.adjustment_brightness = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _set_contrast(value: float) -> void:

		_environment.adjustment_enabled = true
		_environment.adjustment_contrast = value
		_queue_persistent_save()

func _set_glow_enabled(value: bool) -> void:

		_environment,
		_directional_light,
		_base_ambient_color,
		_base_fog_color,
		_base_light_color,
		_warmth
	)

func _apply_sun_preset(preset_name: String) -> void:
	var values := DeveloperEnvironmentPresets.build_state(preset_name, _default_values)
	if values.is_empty():
		return

	_apply_state_values(values)

func _capture_current_values() -> Dictionary:
	return DeveloperEnvironmentState.capture_current_values(_environment, _directional_light, _warmth)

func _apply_state_values(values: Dictionary) -> void:
	_warmth = DeveloperEnvironmentState.apply_state_values(
		_environment,
		_directional_light,
		_default_values,
		_base_ambient_color,
		_base_fog_color,
		_base_light_color,
		values
	)
	environment_state_changed.emit()

func _queue_persistent_save() -> void:
	if _save_timer == null:
		return
	_save_timer.start()
	environment_state_changed.emit()
	_set_status("Saving developer preset...")

func _save_persistent_state() -> void:
	var values := _capture_current_values()
	var save_error := DeveloperEnvironmentPersistence.save_values(values)
	if save_error != OK:
		_set_status("Save failed: %s" % error_string(save_error))
		return

	_set_status("Saved developer preset to user://")

func _load_persistent_state() -> bool:
	var values := DeveloperEnvironmentPersistence.load_values(_default_values.keys())
	_apply_state_values(values)
	return values.size() > 0

func _refresh_editor_preview_from_saved_state(force: bool = false) -> void:
	var signature := DeveloperEnvironmentPersistence.get_file_signature()
	if not force and signature == _editor_persistent_signature:
		return

	_editor_persistent_signature = signature
	if signature.is_empty():
		_apply_state_values(_default_values)
		return

	var values := DeveloperEnvironmentPersistence.load_values(_default_values.keys())
	if values.is_empty():
		_apply_state_values(_default_values)
		return

	_apply_state_values(values)

func _set_status(text_value: String) -> void:
	if _status_label == null:
		return
	_status_label.text = text_value

===FILE: res://scripts/debug/developer_environment_persistence.gd===
class_name DeveloperEnvironmentPersistence
extends RefCounted

const SETTINGS_PATH := "user://developer_environment_settings.cfg"
const SETTINGS_SECTION := "developer_environment"

static func save_values(values: Dictionary, path: String = SETTINGS_PATH, section: String = SETTINGS_SECTION) -> int:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	for key in values.keys():
		config.set_value(section, str(key), values[key])
	return config.save(path)

static func load_values(keys: Array, path: String = SETTINGS_PATH, section: String = SETTINGS_SECTION) -> Dictionary:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	var load_error := config.load(path)
	if load_error != OK:
		return {}

	var values := {}
	for key in keys:
		if config.has_section_key(section, str(key)):
			values[key] = config.get_value(section, str(key))
	return values

static func get_file_signature(path: String = SETTINGS_PATH) -> String:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return ""

	var file := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return ""

	return file.get_as_text()
===END_FILE===
